- **lead/change-manage**: Change management plan for an organisational initiative: stakeholder analysis, communication cadence, resistance handling, timeline and risk mitigations. SKIP: designing the new team structure itself -&gt; org/org-design (structure first, change plan second); interpersonal friction between two people -&gt; lead/conflict-resolve (person-level, not initiative-level). ([andreagroferreira/arka-os](https://github.com/andreagroferreira/arka-os/tree/HEAD/departments/leadership/skills/change-manage) · ★16)
- **lead/conflict-resolve**: Conflict resolution between people: reads both parties' DISC profiles and produces a resolution path with a communication strategy for each side. TRIGGER: "conflito na equipa", "estes dois não se entendem", "resolve conflict", "mediar uma discussão", "team members clashing", "/lead conflict &lt;situation&gt;". SKIP: profiling one person's behavioural style without an active conflict -&gt; lead/disc-assess (assessment, not mediation); systemic team-wide friction -&gt; lead/team-health (diagnoses the five dysfunctions, not a single dispute). ([andreagroferreira/arka-os](https://github.com/andreagroferreira/arka-os/tree/HEAD/departments/leadership/skills/conflict-resolve) · ★16)
- **lead/culture-audit**: Culture audit: assesses values lived vs stated, team health and feedback loops, producing a report with health scores, gaps and improvement actions. TRIGGER: "auditoria à cultura", "a cultura está a degradar-se", "culture audit", "vivemos os nossos valores?", "values vs reality", "/lead culture". SKIP: defining values and culture from scratch -&gt; org/culture-define (creates the culture; this skill audits an existing one); trust/conflict diagnosis of one team -&gt; lead/team-health (Lencioni pyramid, not company-wide culture). ([andreagroferreira/arka-os](https://github.com/andreagroferreira/arka-os/tree/HEAD/departments/leadership/skills/culture-audit) · ★16)
- **lead/delegation-matrix**: Delegation analysis: decides what to delegate, to whom, and with which authority level, producing a delegation matrix with check-in cadence per task. TRIGGER: "o que devo delegar", "estou sobrecarregado, delega isto", "delegation matrix", "níveis de autoridade", "who should own this task", "/lead delegate &lt;task&gt;". SKIP: deciding how decisions get made across the org -&gt; org/decision-framework (decision rights, not task handoff); hiring someone new to absorb the work -&gt; lead/hiring-plan (role creation, not delegation of existing work). ([andreagroferreira/arka-os](https://github.com/andreagroferreira/arka-os/tree/HEAD/departments/leadership/skills/delegation-matrix) · ★16)
- **lead/disc-assess**: DISC behavioural assessment of a person or team: identifies the style and produces a profile with communication recommendations and conflict-resolution tips. TRIGGER: "perfil DISC", "avalia o estilo comportamental", "DISC assessment", "como comunico com ele", "adapt my communication style", "/lead disc &lt;person/team&gt;". SKIP: an active dispute between two people -&gt; lead/conflict-resolve (mediation that uses DISC, not the assessment itself); whole-team dysfunction signals -&gt; lead/team-health (Lencioni model beats individual profiling). ([andreagroferreira/arka-os](https://github.com/andreagroferreira/arka-os/tree/HEAD/departments/leadership/skills/disc-assess) · ★16)
- **lead/feedback-give**: Structured feedback with Radical Candor (care personally + challenge directly): specific, immediate, behavioural — situation, behaviour, impact and follow-up actions. TRIGGER: "dá feedback à equipa", "como digo isto ao colaborador", "give feedback", "feedback difícil", "radical candor", "/lead feedback &lt;person&gt;". SKIP: formal cycle evaluation with evidence and a development plan -&gt; lead/performance-review (periodic review, not in-the-moment feedback); adapting to the person's behavioural style before the conversation -&gt; lead/disc-assess. ([andreagroferreira/arka-os](https://github.com/andreagroferreira/arka-os/tree/HEAD/departments/leadership/skills/feedback-give) · ★16)
- **lead/hiring-plan**: Hiring plan with the Who Method (Geoff Smart): role scorecard, sourcing strategy, structured interview guide, evaluation rubric and sell plan. TRIGGER: "hiring plan", "vamos contratar um developer", "plano de contratação", "scorecard para a vaga", "structured interview questions", "/lead hiring &lt;role&gt;". - **lead/okr-define**: Defines OKRs with John Doerr's framework, cascading company -&gt; team: qualitative Objectives, quantitative Key Results, scoring criteria and check-in schedule. - **lead/performance-review**: Performance review preparation: gathers evidence, structures feedback points and builds a development plan for the review conversation. - **leadbay_build_campaign**: Build a sales campaign from scratch, autonomously, to a target size: discover on the lens, qualify, and enrich the buyer titles until `count` leads each have a reachable target-title contact — no pauses, no confirm gates. Saves via `leadbay_create_campaign` and renders a one-tap call/email view via `leadbay_campaign_call_sheet`. Trigger on "build me a campaign", "build N leads", "create a campaign from scratch". Work an existing one with `leadbay_work_campaign`. ([leadbay/mcp](https://github.com/leadbay/mcp/tree/HEAD/.claude-plugin/plugins/leadbay/skills/leadbay_build_campaign))
- **leadbay_daily_check_in**: Morning DISCOVERY workflow — new leads from the lens wishlist. Trigger on "show me leads", "what's new today", "let's prospect", "run my check-in", "my morning check-in", "I do this every day", "every morning". Recurrence language always means this prompt. Do NOT trigger on follow-up phrasings ("follow up", "before my trip") — those go to `leadbay_followup_check_in`. ([leadbay/mcp](https://github.com/leadbay/mcp/tree/HEAD/.claude-plugin/plugins/leadbay/skills/leadbay_daily_check_in))
- **leadbay_extend_my_lens**: Add more leads to the current lens on demand — for users whose appetite exceeds the standard daily fill. The agent picks seeds silently from what's already on the lens, fires the extra refill, and surfaces the queue confirmation. The user never reviews the seed list. ([leadbay/mcp](https://github.com/leadbay/mcp/tree/HEAD/.claude-plugin/plugins/leadbay/skills/leadbay_extend_my_lens))
- **leadbay_followup_check_in**: Follow-up check-in: surface KNOWN leads from the Monitor view needing re-engagement. Trigger on "follow up", "already known leads", "what's overdue", "before my trip", "who should I re-engage". Do NOT trigger on "show me today's leads", "my morning check-in", "run my check-in", "I do this every day", "every morning" — those go to `leadbay_daily_check_in`. ([leadbay/mcp](https://github.com/leadbay/mcp/tree/HEAD/.claude-plugin/plugins/leadbay/skills/leadbay_followup_check_in))
- **leadbay_getting_started**: Guided first-run walkthrough — four clicks that actually use Leadbay: check the account, pull today's leads, draft a first email to the top one, then reveal who to send it to. Use when the user is new or asks to be SHOWN how Leadbay works ("walk me through Leadbay", "I'm new", "how do I use this", "give me a tour"). Don't use it for orientation prose with no clicking — that's leadbay_prospecting_overview. ([leadbay/mcp](https://github.com/leadbay/mcp/tree/HEAD/.claude-plugin/plugins/leadbay/skills/leadbay_getting_started))
- **leadbay_import_file**: Import a user-supplied CSV/file into Leadbay through five phases with evidence gates — scan, derive, resolve identities, preserve & commit, then optionally qualify and report. The job is to maximize how many rows the Leadbay system actually ingests and matches. ([leadbay/mcp](https://github.com/leadbay/mcp/tree/HEAD/.claude-plugin/plugins/leadbay/skills/leadbay_import_file))
- **leadbay_log_outreach**: Log outreach (an email I sent, a call I made, a meeting I had) on a specific lead. Captures verification so the SDR pipeline trusts the entry. ([leadbay/mcp](https://github.com/leadbay/mcp/tree/HEAD/.claude-plugin/plugins/leadbay/skills/leadbay_log_outreach))
- **leadbay_new_leads**: Guided net-new lead delivery — turn a described need ("gyms around Dallas that would buy our flooring") into ICP-perfect NEW companies with qualification evidence and the right contact, via leadbay_find_new_leads. Trigger when the user DESCRIBES who they want: "get me N companies that &lt;profile&gt;", "we're entering &lt;market&gt;". A bare "find me new leads" with no profile, and "today's leads", are the daily lens batch — leadbay_pull_leads. "Qualify these companies I have" is leadbay_qualify_leads. ([leadbay/mcp](https://github.com/leadbay/mcp/tree/HEAD/.claude-plugin/plugins/leadbay/skills/leadbay_new_leads))
- **leadbay_plan_tour_in_city**: Use whenever the user names a city they'll be in and asks who to see — "I'm in SF next Tuesday, who's worth meeting?", "I'm going to Berlin — who should I visit?", "plan my &lt;city&gt; tour". Any in-person/visit intent tied to a place routes here, NOT to `leadbay_pull_leads`. It surfaces follow-ups + fresh Discover leads in the city via `leadbay_tour_plan`, ALWAYS offers to plot them on a map (rendering it on yes), then offers outreach drafts + campaign persistence. ([leadbay/mcp](https://github.com/leadbay/mcp/tree/HEAD/.claude-plugin/plugins/leadbay/skills/leadbay_plan_tour_in_city))
- **leadbay_prospecting_overview**: Orientation for working with Leadbay from any host — discovery vs. follow-up, the outreach loop, outcome recording, imports, pushback / snooze, and the connected-outreach-tool registry. Trigger when the conversation involves Leadbay leads, prospecting, pipeline, follow-up, outreach, or lens / ICP — anything from "show me my leads" to "what should I follow up on" to "I'll send via lemlist". ([leadbay/mcp](https://github.com/leadbay/mcp/tree/HEAD/.claude-plugin/plugins/leadbay/skills/leadbay_prospecting_overview))
- **leadbay_qualify_top_n**: Bulk-qualify the top N un-qualified leads in the active lens. Uses leadbay_bulk_qualify_leads with a sensible default budget. ([leadbay/mcp](https://github.com/leadbay/mcp/tree/HEAD/.claude-plugin/plugins/leadbay/skills/leadbay_qualify_top_n))
- **leadbay_refine_audience**: Refine the kind of leads Leadbay surfaces beyond firmographics, with a free-text instruction. Handles the clarification round-trip if the new prompt is ambiguous. ([leadbay/mcp](https://github.com/leadbay/mcp/tree/HEAD/.claude-plugin/plugins/leadbay/skills/leadbay_refine_audience))
- **leadbay_research_a_domain**: Resolve a company by name or domain across the user's visible Discover, Monitor, and Activate corpus, then return everything Leadbay knows about it. ([leadbay/mcp](https://github.com/leadbay/mcp/tree/HEAD/.claude-plugin/plugins/leadbay/skills/leadbay_research_a_domain))
- **leadbay_setup_team_prospecting**: Manager-led prospecting setup: conversationally turn a natural-language audience ask into a Leadbay lens, validate the candidate leads, and persist them as one or more named campaigns the rep(s) can work through. Closes #3630 US3 end-to-end (within the current creator-scoped campaign visibility model). ([leadbay/mcp](https://github.com/leadbay/mcp/tree/HEAD/.claude-plugin/plugins/leadbay/skills/leadbay_setup_team_prospecting))
- **leadbay_sync_outreach**: Keep Leadbay's record of who was contacted current: read my mailbox and calendar, log every email and meeting with a Leadbay lead on the person it involved, then schedule the same sync to run every day. Trigger on "log my emails in Leadbay", "sync my outreach", "keep Leadbay up to date with my emails". ([leadbay/mcp](https://github.com/leadbay/mcp/tree/HEAD/.claude-plugin/plugins/leadbay/skills/leadbay_sync_outreach))
- **leadbay_top_accounts_to_activate**: Build a ranked account-conquest plan from Leadbay data — the accounts worth activating, each with a motif, a pitch and a checklist, ranked by the strongest Leadbay signal. Every figure carries its source, and anything Leadbay can't measure is shown as OMITTED rather than estimated. Uses `leadbay_bulk_qualify_leads` and `leadbay_enrich_titles`.
